                    },
                    "pt_position": 1,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "Patient must have previously received treatment under the PBS S100 Growth Hormone Program under the short stature and slow growth category",

          {

            "prescribing_text": {
              "criteria": {
                "criteria_type": "CLINICAL",
                "criteria_parameters": [
                  {
                    "parameter": {
                      "parameter_type": "CLINICAL_PATIENT"
                    },
                    "pt_position": 1,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "Patient must have had a lapse in growth hormone treatment",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
                      "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
                      "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
                    },
                    "parameter_prescribing_txt_id": 16127
                  }
                ],
                "parameter_relationship": "ANY"
              },
              "prescribing_type": "CRITERIA",
              "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 16128
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 4,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "criteria": {
                "criteria_type": "CLINICAL",
                "criteria_parameters": [
                  {
                    "parameter": {
                      "parameter_type": "CLINICAL_TREATMENT"
                    },
                    "pt_position": 1,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "The treatment must not have lapsed due to failure to respond to growth hormone at a dose of 7.5mg/m2/week or greater for the most recent treatment period (32 weeks for an initial or recommencement treatment period and 26 weeks for a continuing treatment period, whichever applies)",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
                      "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
                      "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
                    },
                    "parameter_prescribing_txt_id": 17232
                  },
                  {
                    "parameter": {
                      "parameter_type": "CLINICAL_TREATMENT"
                    },
                    "pt_position": 2,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "The treatment must not have lapsed due to failure to respond to growth hormone at a dose of 7.5mg/m2/week or greater for the most recent treatment period (32 weeks for an initial or recommencement treatment period and 26 weeks for a continuing treatment period, whichever applies), unless response was affected by a significant medical illness",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
                      "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
                      "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
                    },
                    "parameter_prescribing_txt_id": 17233
                  },
                  {
                    "parameter": {
                      "parameter_type": "CLINICAL_TREATMENT"
                    },
                    "pt_position": 3,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "The treatment must not have lapsed due to failure to respond to growth hormone at a dose of 7.5mg/m2/week or greater for the most recent treatment period (32 weeks for an initial or recommencement treatment period and 26 weeks for a continuing treatment period, whichever applies), unless response was affected by major surgery (e.g. renal transplant)",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
                      "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
                      "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
                    },
                    "parameter_prescribing_txt_id": 17234
                  },
                  {
                    "parameter": {
                      "parameter_type": "CLINICAL_TREATMENT"
                    },
                    "pt_position": 4,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "The treatment must not have lapsed due to failure to respond to growth hormone at a dose of 7.5mg/m2/week or greater for the most recent treatment period (32 weeks for an initial or recommencement treatment period and 26 weeks for a continuing treatment period, whichever applies), unless response was affected by an adverse reaction to growth hormone",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
                      "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
                      "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
                    },
                    "parameter_prescribing_txt_id": 17235
                  },
                  {
                    "parameter": {
                      "parameter_type": "CLINICAL_TREATMENT"
                    },
                    "pt_position": 5,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "The treatment must not have lapsed due to failure to respond to growth hormone at a dose of 7.5mg/m2/week or greater for the most recent treatment period (32 weeks for an initial or recommencement treatment period and 26 weeks for a continuing treatment period, whichever applies), unless response was affected by non-compliance due to social/family problems",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
                      "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
                      "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"

                    "parameter_prescribing_txt_id": 17236

              },
              "prescribing_type": "CRITERIA",
              "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 29455
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 11,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "The maximum duration of each recommencement treatment phase is 32 weeks. Prescribers must determine an appropriate weekly dose in accordance with the dosing arrangements detailed in the National Health (Growth Hormone Program) Special Arrangement 2025 and request the appropriate number of vials/cartridges required to provide sufficient drug for 16 weeks' worth of treatment (with up to 1 repeat allowed).",
              "prescribing_type": "PRESCRIBING_INSTRUCTIONS",
              "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 34121
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 12,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "The authority application must be in writing and must include: 1. Details of the proposed prescription; AND 2. A completed Growth Hormone Authority Application Supporting Information Form for recommencement of treatment; AND 3. Recent growth data (height and weight, not older than three months); AND 4. A bone age result performed within the last 12 months (except for a patient whose chronological age is 2.5 years or less); AND 5. The proprietary name (brand), form and strength of somatropin requested, and the number of vials/cartridges required to provide sufficient drug for 16 weeks' worth of treatment (with up to 1 repeat allowed).",
              "prescribing_type": "PRESCRIBING_INSTRUCTIONS",
              "assessment_type_code": "FULL",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 34213
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 13,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "Prescribers must keep a copy of any clinical records relating to the prescription, including such records required to demonstrate that the prescription was written in compliance with any relevant circumstances and/or purposes. These records must be kept for 2 years after the date the prescription to which the records relate is written.",
              "prescribing_type": "PRESCRIBING_INSTRUCTIONS",
              "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 16016
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 14,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "In children with diabetes mellitus prescribers must ascertain that a growth failure is not due to poor diabetes control, diabetes control is adequate, and regular screening occurs for diabetes complications, particularly retinopathy.",
              "prescribing_type": "PRESCRIBING_INSTRUCTIONS",
              "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 28759
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 15,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "If recommencement of treatment is sought under a different indication than that under which the patient was previously receiving treatment an application for recommencement of treatment as a reclassified patient should be submitted.",
              "prescribing_type": "ADMINISTRATIVE_ADVICE",
              "assessment_type_code": null,
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"

                    "prescribing_text": {

                    },
                    "pt_position": 2,

                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",

                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",

              },
              "prescribing_type": "CRITERIA",
              "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",

            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 16426
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 9,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "The maximum duration of each recommencement treatment phase is 32 weeks. Prescribers must determine an appropriate weekly dose in accordance with the dosing arrangements detailed in the National Health (Growth Hormone Program) Special Arrangement 2025 and request the appropriate number of vials/cartridges required to provide sufficient drug for 16 weeks' worth of treatment (with up to 1 repeat allowed).",
              "prescribing_type": "PRESCRIBING_INSTRUCTIONS",
              "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 34121
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 10,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "The authority application must be in writing and must include: 1. Details of the proposed prescription; AND 2. A completed Growth Hormone Authority Application Supporting Information Form for recommencement of treatment; AND 3. Recent growth data (height and weight, not older than three months); AND 4. A bone age result performed within the last 12 months (except for a patient whose chronological age is 2.5 years or less); AND 5. The proprietary name (brand), form and strength of somatropin requested, and the number of vials/cartridges required to provide sufficient drug for 16 weeks' worth of treatment (with up to 1 repeat allowed).",
              "prescribing_type": "PRESCRIBING_INSTRUCTIONS",
              "assessment_type_code": "FULL",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 34214
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 11,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "Prescribers must keep a copy of any clinical records relating to the prescription, including such records required to demonstrate that the prescription was written in compliance with any relevant circumstances and/or purposes. These records must be kept for 2 years after the date the prescription to which the records relate is written.",
              "prescribing_type": "PRESCRIBING_INSTRUCTIONS",
              "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 16016
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 12,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "In children with diabetes mellitus prescribers must ascertain that a growth failure is not due to poor diabetes control, diabetes control is adequate, and regular screening occurs for diabetes complications, particularly retinopathy.",
              "prescribing_type": "PRESCRIBING_INSTRUCTIONS",
              "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 28759
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 13,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "If recommencement of treatment is sought under a different indication than that under which the patient was previously receiving treatment an application for recommencement of treatment as a reclassified patient should be submitted.",
              "prescribing_type": "ADMINISTRATIVE_ADVICE",
              "assessment_type_code": null,
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 16513
          }
        ]

                    },
                    "pt_position": 1,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "Must be treated by a medical practitioner in consultation with a nominated specialist or consultant physician in paediatric endocrinology",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
                      "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
                      "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
                    },
                    "parameter_prescribing_txt_id": 16422
                  },
                  {
                    "parameter": {
                      "parameter_type": "TREATMENT_PRESCRIBER"
                    },
                    "pt_position": 2,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "Must be treated by a medical practitioner in consultation with a nominated specialist or consultant physician in general paediatrics",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
                      "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
                      "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
                    },
                    "parameter_prescribing_txt_id": 16425
                  }
                ],
                "parameter_relationship": "ANY"
              },
              "prescribing_type": "CRITERIA",
              "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 16426
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 9,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "The maximum duration of each recommencement treatment phase is 32 weeks. Prescribers must determine an appropriate weekly dose in accordance with the dosing arrangements detailed in the National Health (Growth Hormone Program) Special Arrangement 2025 and request the appropriate number of vials/cartridges required to provide sufficient drug for 16 weeks' worth of treatment (with up to 1 repeat allowed).",
              "prescribing_type": "PRESCRIBING_INSTRUCTIONS",
              "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 34121
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 10,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "The authority application must be in writing and must include: 1. Details of the proposed prescription; AND 2. A completed Growth Hormone Authority Application Supporting Information Form for recommencement of treatment; AND 3. Recent growth data (height and weight, not older than three months); AND 4. A bone age result performed within the last 12 months (except for a patient whose chronological age is 2.5 years or less); AND 5. The proprietary name (brand), form and strength of somatropin requested, and the number of vials/cartridges required to provide sufficient drug for 16 weeks' worth of treatment (with up to 1 repeat allowed).",
              "prescribing_type": "PRESCRIBING_INSTRUCTIONS",
              "assessment_type_code": "FULL",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 34214
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 11,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "Prescribers must keep a copy of any clinical records relating to the prescription, including such records required to demonstrate that the prescription was written in compliance with any relevant circumstances and/or purposes. These records must be kept for 2 years after the date the prescription to which the records relate is written.",
              "prescribing_type": "PRESCRIBING_INSTRUCTIONS",
              "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 16016
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 12,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "In children with diabetes mellitus prescribers must ascertain that a growth failure is not due to poor diabetes control, diabetes control is adequate, and regular screening occurs for diabetes complications, particularly retinopathy.",
              "prescribing_type": "PRESCRIBING_INSTRUCTIONS",
              "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 28759
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 13,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "If recommencement of treatment is sought under a different indication than that under which the patient was previously receiving treatment an application for recommencement of treatment as a reclassified patient should be submitted.",
              "prescribing_type": "ADMINISTRATIVE_ADVICE",
              "assessment_type_code": null,
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",

                    },
                    "pt_position": 2,

                      "prescribing_txt": "Must be treated by a medical practitioner in consultation with a nominated specialist or consultant physician in general paediatrics",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",

              },
              "prescribing_type": "CRITERIA",
              "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",

            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 16426
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 9,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "The maximum duration of each recommencement treatment phase is 32 weeks. Prescribers must determine an appropriate weekly dose in accordance with the dosing arrangements detailed in the National Health (Growth Hormone Program) Special Arrangement 2025 and request the appropriate number of vials/cartridges required to provide sufficient drug for 16 weeks' worth of treatment (with up to 1 repeat allowed).",
              "prescribing_type": "PRESCRIBING_INSTRUCTIONS",
              "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 34121
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 10,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "The authority application must be in writing and must include: 1. Details of the proposed prescription; AND 2. A completed Growth Hormone Authority Application Supporting Information Form for recommencement of treatment; AND 3. Recent growth data (height and weight, not older than three months); AND 4. A bone age result performed within the last 12 months (except for a patient whose chronological age is 2.5 years or less); AND 5. The proprietary name (brand), form and strength of somatropin requested, and the number of vials/cartridges required to provide sufficient drug for 16 weeks' worth of treatment (with up to 1 repeat allowed).",
              "prescribing_type": "PRESCRIBING_INSTRUCTIONS",
              "assessment_type_code": "FULL",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 34214
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 11,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "Prescribers must keep a copy of any clinical records relating to the prescription, including such records required to demonstrate that the prescription was written in compliance with any relevant circumstances and/or purposes. These records must be kept for 2 years after the date the prescription to which the records relate is written.",
              "prescribing_type": "PRESCRIBING_INSTRUCTIONS",
              "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 16016
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 12,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "In children with diabetes mellitus prescribers must ascertain that a growth failure is not due to poor diabetes control, diabetes control is adequate, and regular screening occurs for diabetes complications, particularly retinopathy.",
              "prescribing_type": "PRESCRIBING_INSTRUCTIONS",
              "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 28759
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 13,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "If recommencement of treatment is sought under a different indication than that under which the patient was previously receiving treatment an application for recommencement of treatment as a reclassified patient should be submitted.",
              "prescribing_type": "ADMINISTRATIVE_ADVICE",
              "assessment_type_code": null,
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 16513
          }
        ]

          },
          {
            "pt_position": 10,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "The maximum duration of each recommencement treatment phase is 32 weeks. Prescribers must determine an appropriate weekly dose in accordance with the dosing arrangements detailed in the National Health (Growth Hormone Program) Special Arrangement 2025 and request the appropriate number of vials/cartridges required to provide sufficient drug for 16 weeks' worth of treatment (with up to 1 repeat allowed).",
              "prescribing_type": "PRESCRIBING_INSTRUCTIONS",
              "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 34121
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 11,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "The authority application must be in writing and must include: 1. Details of the proposed prescription; AND 2. A completed Growth Hormone Authority Application Supporting Information Form for recommencement of treatment; AND 3. Recent growth data (height and weight, not older than three months); AND 4. A bone age result performed within the last 12 months (except for a patient whose chronological age is 2.5 years or less); AND 5. The proprietary name (brand), form and strength of somatropin requested, and the number of vials/cartridges required to provide sufficient drug for 16 weeks' worth of treatment (with up to 1 repeat allowed).",
              "prescribing_type": "PRESCRIBING_INSTRUCTIONS",
              "assessment_type_code": "FULL",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 34213
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 12,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "Prescribers must keep a copy of any clinical records relating to the prescription, including such records required to demonstrate that the prescription was written in compliance with any relevant circumstances and/or purposes. These records must be kept for 2 years after the date the prescription to which the records relate is written.",
              "prescribing_type": "PRESCRIBING_INSTRUCTIONS",
              "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 16016
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 13,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "In children with diabetes mellitus prescribers must ascertain that a growth failure is not due to poor diabetes control, diabetes control is adequate, and regular screening occurs for diabetes complications, particularly retinopathy.",
              "prescribing_type": "PRESCRIBING_INSTRUCTIONS",
              "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 28759
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 14,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "If recommencement of treatment is sought under a different indication than that under which the patient was previously receiving treatment an application for recommencement of treatment as a reclassified patient should be submitted.",
              "prescribing_type": "ADMINISTRATIVE_ADVICE",
              "assessment_type_code": null,
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"

              "prescribing_txt": "The authority application must be in writing and must include: 1. Details of the proposed prescription; AND 2. A completed Growth Hormone Authority Application Supporting Information Form for recommencement of treatment; AND 3. Recent growth data (height and weight, not older than three months); AND 4. A bone age result performed within the last 12 months (except for a patient whose chronological age is 2.5 years or less); AND 5. The proprietary name (brand), form and strength of somatropin requested, and the number of vials/cartridges required to provide sufficient drug for 16 weeks' worth of treatment (with up to 1 repeat allowed).",

            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "Prescribers must keep a copy of any clinical records relating to the prescription, including such records required to demonstrate that the prescription was written in compliance with any relevant circumstances and/or purposes.           },
          {
            "pt_position": 11,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "The maximum duration of each recommencement treatment phase is 32 weeks. Prescribers must determine an appropriate weekly dose in accordance with the dosing arrangements detailed in the National Health (Growth Hormone Program) Special Arrangement 2025 and request the appropriate number of vials/cartridges required to provide sufficient drug for 16 weeks' worth of treatment (with up to 1 repeat allowed).",
              "prescribing_type": "PRESCRIBING_INSTRUCTIONS",
              "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 34121
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 12,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "The authority application must be in writing and must include: 1. Details of the proposed prescription; AND 2. A completed Growth Hormone Authority Application Supporting Information Form for recommencement of treatment; AND 3. Recent growth data (height, weight, and waist circumference, not older than three months); AND 4. The date at which skeletal maturity was achieved (if applicable) [Note: In patients whose chronological age is greater than 2.5 years, a bone age reading should be performed at least once every 12 months prior to attainment of skeletal maturity.]; AND 5. Confirmation that during the initial 32 week treatment period, the patient was re-evaluated via polysomnography for airway obstruction and apnoea, and any sleep disorders that were identified have been addressed; AND 6. The proprietary name (brand), form and strength of somatropin requested, and the number of vials/cartridges required to provide sufficient drug for 16 weeks' worth of treatment (with up to 1 repeat allowed).",
              "prescribing_type": "PRESCRIBING_INSTRUCTIONS",
              "assessment_type_code": "FULL",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 34240
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 13,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "Prescribers must keep a copy of any clinical records relating to the prescription, including such records required to demonstrate that the prescription was written in compliance with any relevant circumstances and/or purposes. These records must be kept for 2 years after the date the prescription to which the records relate is written.",
              "prescribing_type": "PRESCRIBING_INSTRUCTIONS",
              "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 16016
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 14,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "In children with diabetes mellitus prescribers must ascertain that a growth failure is not due to poor diabetes control, diabetes control is adequate, and regular screening occurs for diabetes complications, particularly retinopathy.",

              "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",

                    "parameter": {

                    },
                    "pt_position": 1,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "The treatment must not have lapsed due to failure to respond to growth hormone at a dose of 7.5mg/m2/week or greater for the most recent treatment period (32 weeks for an initial or recommencement treatment period and 26 weeks for a continuing treatment period, whichever applies)",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
                      "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
                      "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"

                  },
                  {
                    "parameter": {
                      "parameter_type": "CLINICAL_TREATMENT"
                    },
                    "pt_position": 2,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "The treatment must not have lapsed due to failure to respond to growth hormone at a dose of 7.5mg/m2/week or greater for the most recent treatment period (32 weeks for an initial or recommencement treatment period and 26 weeks for a continuing treatment period, whichever applies), unless response was affected by a significant medical illness",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
                      "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
                      "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
                    },
                    "parameter_prescribing_txt_id": 17233
                  },
                  {
                    "parameter": {

                    },
                    "pt_position": 3,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "The treatment must not have lapsed due to failure to respond to growth hormone at a dose of 7.5mg/m2/week or greater for the most recent treatment period (32 weeks for an initial or recommencement treatment period and 26 weeks for a continuing treatment period, whichever applies), unless response was affected by major surgery (e.g. renal transplant)",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
                      "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
                      "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"

                    },
                    "pt_position": 4,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "The treatment must not have lapsed due to failure to respond to growth hormone at a dose of 7.5mg/m2/week or greater for the most recent treatment period (32 weeks for an initial or recommencement treatment period and 26 weeks for a continuing treatment period, whichever applies), unless response was affected by an adverse reaction to growth hormone",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
                      "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",

                    },
                    "pt_position": 5,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "The treatment must not have lapsed due to failure to respond to growth hormone at a dose of 7.5mg/m2/week or greater for the most recent treatment period (32 weeks for an initial or recommencement treatment period and 26 weeks for a continuing treatment period, whichever applies), unless response was affected by non-compliance due to social/family problems",

                "criteria_parameters": [
                  {
                    "parameter": {
                      "parameter_type": "CLINICAL_PATIENT"
                    },
                    "pt_position": 1,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "Patient must have previously received treatment under the indication short stature associated with chronic renal insufficiency, have undergone a renal transplant and a 12 month period of observation following the transplant, and have an estimated glomerular filtration rate of greater than or equal to 30mL/minute/1.73m2 measured by creatinine clearance, excretion of radionuclides such as DTPA, or by the height/creatinine formula",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
                      "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",

                    },

                  },
                  {
                    "parameter": {
                      "parameter_type": "CLINICAL_PATIENT"
                    },
                    "pt_position": 2,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "Patient must have had a height at or below the 1st percentile for age and sex immediately prior to commencing treatment and a growth velocity below the 25th percentile for bone age and sex measured over the 12 month interval immediately prior to commencement of treatment (or the 6 month interval immediately prior to commencement of treatment if the patient was an older child at commencement of treatment)",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
                      "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
                      "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
                    },
                    "parameter_prescribing_txt_id": 23448
                  },
                  {
                    "parameter": {
                      "parameter_type": "CLINICAL_PATIENT"
                    },
                    "pt_position": 3,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "Patient must have had both: (i) a height no higher than the 1st percentile for age plus sex at the time of having commenced treatment with this drug, (ii) over the 12 month interval immediately prior to having commenced treatment, a growth velocity no greater than 8 cm/year where the patient had a bone/chronological age of no greater than 2.5 years",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
                      "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
                      "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
                    },
                    "parameter_prescribing_txt_id": 29805

                    "pt_position": 1,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "Patient must be undergoing treatment for the stated indication with only one growth hormone at any given time",

            },

          },
          {
            "pt_position": 12,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "An older child is defined as a male with a chronological age of at least 12 years or a bone age of at least 10 years, or a female with a chronological age of at least 10 years or a bone age of at least 8 years.",
              "prescribing_type": "PRESCRIBING_INSTRUCTIONS",
              "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 23357
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 13,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "The maximum duration of each recommencement treatment phase is 32 weeks. Prescribers must determine an appropriate weekly dose in accordance with the dosing arrangements detailed in the National Health (Growth Hormone Program) Special Arrangement 2025 and request the appropriate number of vials/cartridges required to provide sufficient drug for 16 weeks' worth of treatment (with up to 1 repeat allowed).",
              "prescribing_type": "PRESCRIBING_INSTRUCTIONS",
              "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 34121
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 14,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "The authority application must be in writing and must include: 1. Details of the proposed prescription; AND 2. A completed Growth Hormone Authority Application Supporting Information Form for recommencement of treatment as a reclassified patient; AND 3. (a) A minimum of 12 months of growth data (height and weight measurements) from immediately prior to commencement of treatment, or a minimum of 6 months of growth data from immediately prior to commencement of treatment if the patient was an older child at commencement of treatment; and the result of a bone age assessment performed within the 12 months immediately prior to commencement of treatment (where the patient's chronological age was higher than 2.5 years); OR (b) Confirmation that the patient has previously received treatment under the indication short stature associated with chronic renal insufficiency, has undergone a renal transplant and a 12 month period of observation following the transplant, and has an estimated glomerular filtration rate of greater than or equal to 30mL/minute/1.73m2 measured by creatinine clearance, excretion of radionuclides such as DTPA, or by the height/creatinine formula; AND 4. Recent growth data (height and weight, not older than three months); AND 5. A bone age result performed within the last 12 months (except for a patient whose chronological age is 2.5 years or less); AND 6. The proprietary name (brand), form and strength of the growth hormone requested, and the number of vials/cartridges required to provide sufficient drug for 16 weeks' worth of treatment (with up to 1 repeat allowed).",
              "prescribing_type": "PRESCRIBING_INSTRUCTIONS",
              "assessment_type_code": "FULL",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 34215
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 15,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "Prescribers must keep a copy of any clinical records relating to the prescription, including such records required to demonstrate that the prescription was written in compliance with any relevant circumstances and/or purposes. These records must be kept for 2 years after the date the prescription to which the records relate is written.",
              "prescribing_type": "PRESCRIBING_INSTRUCTIONS",
              "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 16016
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 16,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "In children with diabetes mellitus prescribers must ascertain that a growth failure is not due to poor diabetes control, diabetes control is adequate, and regular screening occurs for diabetes complications, particularly retinopathy.",
              "prescribing_type": "PRESCRIBING_INSTRUCTIONS",
              "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 28759
          }

          {
            "pt_position": 5,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "criteria": {

                "criteria_parameters": [
                  {
                    "parameter": {
                      "parameter_type": "CLINICAL_PATIENT"
                    },
                    "pt_position": 1,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "Patient must have previously received treatment under the indication risk of hypoglycaemia secondary to growth hormone deficiency in neonates/infants and have reached or surpassed 5 years of age (chronological)",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
                      "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",

                  },
                  {
                    "parameter": {
                      "parameter_type": "CLINICAL_PATIENT"
                    },
                    "pt_position": 2,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "Patient must have had a height at or below the 1st percentile for age and sex immediately prior to commencing treatment",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
                      "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
                      "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
                    },
                    "parameter_prescribing_txt_id": 23410
                  },
                  {
                    "parameter": {
                      "parameter_type": "CLINICAL_PATIENT"
                    },
                    "pt_position": 3,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "Patient must have had both a height above the 1st and at or below the 25th percentiles for age and sex immediately prior to commencing treatment and a growth velocity below the 25th percentile for bone age and sex measured over the 12 month interval immediately prior to commencement of treatment (or the 6 month interval immediately prior to commencement of treatment if the patient was an older child at commencement of treatment)",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
                      "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
                      "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
                    },
                    "parameter_prescribing_txt_id": 23454
                  },
                  {
                    "parameter": {
                      "parameter_type": "CLINICAL_PATIENT"
                    },
                    "pt_position": 4,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "Patient must have had both a height above the 1st and at or below the 25th percentiles for age and sex immediately prior to commencing treatment and an annual growth velocity of 14 cm per year or less in the 12 month period immediately prior to commencement of treatment, if the patient had a chronological age of 2 years or less at commencement of treatment",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
                      "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
                      "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
                    },
                    "parameter_prescribing_txt_id": 23455
                  },
                  {
                    "parameter": {
                      "parameter_type": "CLINICAL_PATIENT"
                    },
                    "pt_position": 5,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "Patient must have had both a height above the 1st and at or below the 25th percentiles for age and sex immediately prior to commencing treatment and an annual growth velocity of 8 cm per year or less in the 12 month period immediately prior to commencement of treatment, if the patient had a bone or chronological age of 2.5 years or less at commencement of treatment",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
                      "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
                      "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
                    },
                    "parameter_prescribing_txt_id": 23456
                  }

                "parameter_relationship": "ANY"

              "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"

                "criteria_type": "CLINICAL",
                "criteria_parameters": [
                  {
                    "parameter": {
                      "parameter_type": "CLINICAL_PATIENT"
                    },
                    "pt_position": 1,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "Patient must have evidence of biochemical growth hormone deficiency, with a peak serum growth hormone concentration less than 10 mU/L or less than or equal to 3.3 micrograms per litre in response to 2 pharmacological growth hormone stimulation tests (e.g. arginine, clonidine, glucagon, insulin)",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
                      "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
                      "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
                    },
                    "parameter_prescribing_txt_id": 23363
                  },
                  {
                    "parameter": {
                      "parameter_type": "CLINICAL_PATIENT"
                    },
                    "pt_position": 2,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "Patient must have evidence of biochemical growth hormone deficiency, with a peak serum growth hormone concentration less than 10 mU/L or less than or equal to 3.3 micrograms per litre in response to 1 pharmacological growth hormone stimulation test (e.g. arginine, clonidine, glucagon, insulin) and 1 physiological growth hormone stimulation test (e.g. sleep, exercise)",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
                      "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
                      "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
                    },
                    "parameter_prescribing_txt_id": 23364
                  },
                  {
                    "parameter": {
                      "parameter_type": "CLINICAL_PATIENT"
                    },
                    "pt_position": 3,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "Patient must have evidence of biochemical growth hormone deficiency, with a peak serum growth hormone concentration less than 10 mU/L or less than or equal to 3.3 micrograms per litre in response to 1 growth hormone stimulation test (pharmacological or physiological e.g. arginine, clonidine, glucagon, insulin, sleep, exercise) with other evidence of growth hormone deficiency, including septo-optic dysplasia (absent corpus callosum and/or septum pellucidum), midline abnormality including optic nerve hypoplasia, cleft lip and palate, midfacial hypoplasia and central incisor, ectopic and/or absent posterior pituitary bright spot, absent empty sella syndrome, hypoplastic anterior pituitary gland and/or pituitary stalk/infundibulum, and genetically proven biochemical growth hormone deficiency either isolated or as part of hypopituitarism in association with pituitary deficits (ACTH, TSH, GnRH or vasopressin/ADH deficiency)",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
                      "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
                      "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
                    },
                    "parameter_prescribing_txt_id": 23365
                  },
                  {

                    },
                    "pt_position": 4,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "Patient must have evidence of biochemical growth hormone deficiency, with a peak serum growth hormone concentration less than 10 mU/L or less than or equal to 3.3 micrograms per litre in response to 1 growth hormone stimulation test (pharmacological or physiological e.g. arginine, clonidine, glucagon, insulin, sleep, exercise) and low plasma IGF-1 levels",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",

                    },
                    "pt_position": 1,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "Patient must be undergoing treatment for the stated indication with only one growth hormone at any given time",

                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",

                "parameter_relationship": "ANY"

              "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 29455
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 12,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "An older child is defined as a male with a chronological age of at least 12 years or a bone age of at least 10 years, or a female with a chronological age of at least 10 years or a bone age of at least 8 years.",
              "prescribing_type": "PRESCRIBING_INSTRUCTIONS",
              "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 23357
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 13,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "The maximum duration of each recommencement treatment phase is 32 weeks. Prescribers must determine an appropriate weekly dose in accordance with the dosing arrangements detailed in the National Health (Growth Hormone Program) Special Arrangement 2025 and request the appropriate number of vials/cartridges required to provide sufficient drug for 16 weeks' worth of treatment (with up to 1 repeat allowed).",
              "prescribing_type": "PRESCRIBING_INSTRUCTIONS",
              "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 34121
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 14,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "The authority application must be in writing and must include: 1. Details of the proposed prescription; AND 2. A completed Growth Hormone Authority Application Supporting Information Form for recommencement of treatment as a reclassified patient; AND 3. (a) A minimum of 12 months of growth data (height and weight measurements) from immediately prior to commencement of treatment, or a minimum of 6 months of growth data from immediately prior to commencement of treatment if the patient was an older child at commencement of treatment; and the result of a bone age assessment performed within the 12 months immediately prior to commencement of treatment (except for a patient whose chronological age was 2.5 years or less at commencement of treatment); OR (b) Height and weight measurements from within three months prior to commencement of treatment for a patient whose height was at or below the 1st percentile for age and sex immediately prior to commencing treatment; OR (c) Confirmation that the patient has previously received treatment under the indication risk of hypoglycaemia secondary to growth hormone deficiency in neonates/infants and has reached or surpassed 5 years of age (chronological); AND 4. Evidence of biochemical growth hormone deficiency, including the type of tests performed and peak growth hormone concentrations; AND 5. Recent growth data (height and weight, not older than three months); AND 6. A bone age result performed within the last 12 months (except for a patient whose chronological age is 2.5 years or less); AND 7. The proprietary name (brand), form and strength of somatropin requested, and the number of vials/cartridges required to provide sufficient drug for 16 weeks' worth of treatment (with up to 1 repeat allowed).",
              "prescribing_type": "PRESCRIBING_INSTRUCTIONS",
              "assessment_type_code": "FULL",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 34216
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 15,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "Prescribers must keep a copy of any clinical records relating to the prescription, including such records required to demonstrate that the prescription was written in compliance with any relevant circumstances and/or purposes. These records must be kept for 2 years after the date the prescription to which the records relate is written.",
              "prescribing_type": "PRESCRIBING_INSTRUCTIONS",
              "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 16016
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 16,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "Biochemical growth hormone deficiency should not be secondary to an intracranial lesion or cranial irradiation for applications under this category.",
              "prescribing_type": "PRESCRIBING_INSTRUCTIONS",
              "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 15905
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 17,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "prescribing_txt": "In children with diabetes mellitus prescribers must ascertain that a growth failure is not due to poor diabetes control, diabetes control is adequate, and regular screening occurs for diabetes complications, particularly retinopathy.",
              "prescribing_type": "PRESCRIBING_INSTRUCTIONS",
              "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 28759
          }
        ]
      }

          },
          {
            "pt_position": 4,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "criteria": {
                "criteria_type": "CLINICAL",
                "criteria_parameters": [
                  {
                    "parameter": {
                      "parameter_type": "CLINICAL_TREATMENT"
                    },
                    "pt_position": 1,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "The treatment must not have lapsed due to failure to respond to growth hormone at a dose of 7.5mg/m2/week or greater for the most recent treatment period (32 weeks for an initial or recommencement treatment period and 26 weeks for a continuing treatment period, whichever applies)",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
                      "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
                      "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
                    },
                    "parameter_prescribing_txt_id": 17232
                  },
                  {
                    "parameter": {
                      "parameter_type": "CLINICAL_TREATMENT"
                    },
                    "pt_position": 2,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "The treatment must not have lapsed due to failure to respond to growth hormone at a dose of 7.5mg/m2/week or greater for the most recent treatment period (32 weeks for an initial or recommencement treatment period and 26 weeks for a continuing treatment period, whichever applies), unless response was affected by a significant medical illness",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
                      "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
                      "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
                    },
                    "parameter_prescribing_txt_id": 17233
                  },
                  {
                    "parameter": {
                      "parameter_type": "CLINICAL_TREATMENT"
                    },
                    "pt_position": 3,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "The treatment must not have lapsed due to failure to respond to growth hormone at a dose of 7.5mg/m2/week or greater for the most recent treatment period (32 weeks for an initial or recommencement treatment period and 26 weeks for a continuing treatment period, whichever applies), unless response was affected by major surgery (e.g. renal transplant)",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
                      "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
                      "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
                    },
                    "parameter_prescribing_txt_id": 17234

                  {

                    },
                    "pt_position": 4,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "The treatment must not have lapsed due to failure to respond to growth hormone at a dose of 7.5mg/m2/week or greater for the most recent treatment period (32 weeks for an initial or recommencement treatment period and 26 weeks for a continuing treatment period, whichever applies), unless response was affected by an adverse reaction to growth hormone",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
                      "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",

                    "pt_position": 5,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "The treatment must not have lapsed due to failure to respond to growth hormone at a dose of 7.5mg/m2/week or greater for the most recent treatment period (32 weeks for an initial or recommencement treatment period and 26 weeks for a continuing treatment period, whichever applies), unless response was affected by non-compliance due to social/family problems",

          {

                    },
                    "pt_position": 1,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "Patient must have had an intracranial lesion which is under appropriate observation and management",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",

                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",

                    },
                    "pt_position": 2,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "Patient must have received cranial irradiation without having had an intracranial lesion, and is under appropriate observation and management",

                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
                      "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",

                    },

                ],
                "parameter_relationship": "ANY"

              "prescribing_type": "CRITERIA",
              "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",

              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
            "prescribing_text_id": 28762
          },
          {
            "pt_position": 6,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "criteria": {
                "criteria_type": "CLINICAL",
                "criteria_parameters": [
                  {
                    "parameter": {
                      "parameter_type": "CLINICAL_PATIENT"
                    },
                    "pt_position": 1,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "Patient must have evidence of biochemical growth hormone deficiency, with a peak serum growth hormone concentration less than 10 mU/L or less than or equal to 3.3 micrograms per litre in response to 2 pharmacological growth hormone stimulation tests (e.g. arginine, clonidine, glucagon, insulin)",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
                      "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
                      "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
                    },
                    "parameter_prescribing_txt_id": 23363
                  },
                  {
                    "parameter": {
                      "parameter_type": "CLINICAL_PATIENT"
                    },
                    "pt_position": 2,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "Patient must have evidence of biochemical growth hormone deficiency, with a peak serum growth hormone concentration less than 10 mU/L or less than or equal to 3.3 micrograms per litre in response to 1 pharmacological growth hormone stimulation test (e.g. arginine, clonidine, glucagon, insulin) and 1 physiological growth hormone stimulation test (e.g. sleep, exercise)",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
                      "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
                      "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
                    },
                    "parameter_prescribing_txt_id": 23364
                  },
                  {
                    "parameter": {
                      "parameter_type": "CLINICAL_PATIENT"
                    },
                    "pt_position": 3,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "Patient must have evidence of biochemical growth hormone deficiency, with a peak serum growth hormone concentration less than 10 mU/L or less than or equal to 3.3 micrograms per litre in response to 1 growth hormone stimulation test (pharmacological or physiological e.g. arginine, clonidine, glucagon, insulin, sleep, exercise) with other evidence of growth hormone deficiency, including septo-optic dysplasia (absent corpus callosum and/or septum pellucidum), midline abnormality including optic nerve hypoplasia, cleft lip and palate, midfacial hypoplasia and central incisor, ectopic and/or absent posterior pituitary bright spot, absent empty sella syndrome, hypoplastic anterior pituitary gland and/or pituitary stalk/infundibulum, and genetically proven biochemical growth hormone deficiency either isolated or as part of hypopituitarism in association with pituitary deficits (ACTH, TSH, GnRH or vasopressin/ADH deficiency)",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
                      "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
                      "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
                    },
                    "parameter_prescribing_txt_id": 23365
                  },
                  {
                    "parameter": {
                      "parameter_type": "CLINICAL_PATIENT"
                    },
                    "pt_position": 4,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "Patient must have evidence of biochemical growth hormone deficiency, with a peak serum growth hormone concentration less than 10 mU/L or less than or equal to 3.3 micrograms per litre in response to 1 growth hormone stimulation test (pharmacological or physiological e.g. arginine, clonidine, glucagon, insulin, sleep, exercise) and low plasma IGF-1 levels",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
                      "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
                      "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
                    },
                    "parameter_prescribing_txt_id": 23366
                  },
                  {
                    "parameter": {
                      "parameter_type": "CLINICAL_PATIENT"
                    },
                    "pt_position": 5,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "Patient must have evidence of biochemical growth hormone deficiency, with a peak serum growth hormone concentration less than 10 mU/L or less than or equal to 3.3 micrograms per litre in response to 1 growth hormone stimulation test (pharmacological or physiological e.g. arginine, clonidine, glucagon, insulin, sleep, exercise) and low plasma IGFBP-3 levels",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
                      "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
                      "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
                    },
                    "parameter_prescribing_txt_id": 23367

                "parameter_relationship": "ANY"

              "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"

          },
          {
            "pt_position": 7,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "criteria": {
                "criteria_type": "CLINICAL",
                "criteria_parameters": [
                  {
                    "parameter": {
                      "parameter_type": "CLINICAL_PATIENT"
                    },
                    "pt_position": 1,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "Patient must have had a height at or below the 1st percentile for age and sex immediately prior to commencing treatment",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
                      "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
                      "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
                    },
                    "parameter_prescribing_txt_id": 23410
                  },
                  {
                    "parameter": {
                      "parameter_type": "CLINICAL_PATIENT"
                    },
                    "pt_position": 2,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "Patient must have had both a height above the 1st percentile for age and sex immediately prior to commencing treatment and a growth velocity below the 25th percentile for bone age and sex measured over the 12 month interval immediately prior to commencement of treatment (or the 6 month interval immediately prior to commencement of treatment if the patient was an older child at commencement of treatment)",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
                      "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
                      "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
                    },
                    "parameter_prescribing_txt_id": 23411
                  },
                  {
                    "parameter": {
                      "parameter_type": "CLINICAL_PATIENT"
                    },
                    "pt_position": 3,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "Patient must have had both a height above the 1st percentile for age and sex immediately prior to commencing treatment and an annual growth velocity of 14 cm per year or less in the 12 month period immediately prior to commencement of treatment, if the patient had a chronological age of 2 years or less at commencement of treatment",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
                      "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
                      "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
                    },
                    "parameter_prescribing_txt_id": 23412
                  },
                  {
                    "parameter": {
                      "parameter_type": "CLINICAL_PATIENT"
                    },
                    "pt_position": 4,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "Patient must have had both a height above the 1st percentile for age and sex immediately prior to commencing treatment and an annual growth velocity of 8 cm per year or less in the 12 month period immediately prior to commencement of treatment, if the patient had a bone or chronological age of 2.5 years or less at commencement of treatment",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
                      "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
                      "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
                    },
                    "parameter_prescribing_txt_id": 23413
                  }
                ],
                "parameter_relationship": "ANY"
              },
              "prescribing_type": "CRITERIA",
              "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },

          },
          {
            "pt_position": 8,
            "prescribing_text": {
              "criteria": {
                "criteria_type": "CLINICAL",
                "criteria_parameters": [
                  {
                    "parameter": {
                      "parameter_type": "CLINICAL_PATIENT"
                    },
                    "pt_position": 1,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "Patient must not have a condition with a known risk of malignancy including chromosomal abnormalities such as Down and Bloom syndromes",

                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",

          },
          {

            "prescribing_text": {
              "criteria": {
                "criteria_type": "CLINICAL",
                "criteria_parameters": [
                  {
                    "parameter": {
                      "parameter_type": "CLINICAL_PATIENT"
                    },
                    "pt_position": 1,
                    "prescribing_text": {
                      "prescribing_txt": "Patient must not have an active tumour or evidence of tumour growth or activity",
                      "prescribing_type": "PARAMETER",
                      "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
                      "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
                      "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
                      "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
                    },
                    "parameter_prescribing_txt_id": 15710

              "assessment_type_code": "IMMEDIATE",
              "apply_to_increase_mq_flag": "N",
              "apply_to_increase_nr_flag": "N",
              "complex_authority_rqrd_ind": "N"
            },
